<h3><a href="https://x.com/EvanLuthra/status/2103087718519718373">JEV: An AI That Doesn&#39;t Chat or Write Hits 18K+ Stars</a></h3>
<p>JEV is an open-source AI model that doesn&#39;t chat or write. You give it a state and a question, it returns a decision, and your code handles the execution. This simple idea is already used across browser agents, coding tools, trading, and tax scenarios.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>JEV 是一个不聊天、不写文章的开源 AI 模型。你给它一个状态和一个问题，它返回一个决策，由你的代码负责执行。这个简单思路已被浏览器代理、编程工具、交易和税务等场景采用。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/QingQ77/status/2103030172542783546">Jev browser tool and Codex: trade-offs of a low-cost click proxy</a></h3>
<p>The post analyzes a division of labor where click operations are delegated to a low-cost model ($0.042 per million input tokens) while Codex handles typing and acceptance, reducing main model call costs. However, the tool only consumes the accessibility tree; canvas, iframe, drag-and-drop, and uploads must be handled manually, and debugging costs depend on how many page elements can be clicked by name.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>帖子分析了将点击操作交给低成本模型（每百万输入token $0.042）、Codex负责打字和验收的分工方式，可减少主模型调用成本；但工具仅支持无障碍树，canvas、iframe、拖拽和上传需自行处理，调试成本取决于页面元素的可用性。</p>
<p><strong>Geek Lite</strong> · @QingQ77 · 2026-09-24</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/sysevolai/status/2102979401025806732">TypeSafe Jev Outperforms Qwen3-Reranker in Code Reranking Test</a></h3>
<p>TypeSafe&#39;s Jev returns typed scores and was tested as a code reranker on 100 real GitHub issues, achieving 71.4% Recall@5 vs 63.4% for dense retrieval plus Qwen3-Reranker-4B, at about the same median latency (4.6s).</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>TypeSafe 的 Jev 模型返回类型化分数，在 100 个真实 GitHub issue 上作为代码重排序器测试，Recall@5 达 71.4%，优于稠密检索加 Qwen3-Reranker-4B 的 63.4%，中位延迟相近（4.6 秒）。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/0xbelorix/status/2102991417392218335">Jev: $0.042 per 1M input tokens, output free</a></h3>
<p>The post compares Claude and Jev: Claude re-reads the whole pile to verify, and each loop adds cost; Jev costs $0.042 per 1M input tokens, output is free, and it returns a decision rather than another essay.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>帖子对比了 Claude 与 Jev：Claude 需要反复读取全部内容来验证，循环越多成本越高；Jev 每百万输入令牌仅需 $0.042，输出免费，直接返回决策，而不是生成更多文本。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/christzolov/status/2102009563083870575">Java SDK and Spring AI Integration for TypeSafe Jev</a></h3>
<p>Java SDK and Spring AI integrations for TypeSafe AI&#39;s Jev. Jev is not a chat model: send it a state and typed questions, and get numbers back with a confidence in about 300 ms, with judge, self-refine and guardrail advisors, RAG triage, tool selection and more.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>为 TypeSafe AI 的 Jev 提供 Java SDK 与 Spring AI 集成。Jev 不是聊天模型：传入状态和类型化问题，约 300 毫秒返回带置信度的数值，并支持评判、自我优化与护栏顾问、RAG 分流、工具选择等。</p>

<p>Laya is an open-source alternative to Jev, positioned as a non-generative decision model that runs at 32.8 ms per query on T4, supports 100+ languages, and is fully free and self-hostable. <p>Simple Jev reuses shared-context KV cache, reads only the next-token logits of candidate labels, and assembles choice, score, and truth judgment server-side, avoiding per-token decoding and JSON generation.</p>
